---
## Intent

Tie each user to one organization, with a role that is meaningful inside that organization. A small fixed set of roles is stored as an enumeration, and the organization is a required association.

### The role belongs to the organization

A role such as owner or member describes what the user may do in its organization. The association to the organization is required, so every user has exactly one organization. The enumeration gives the set of roles a fixed meaning in the model.

### A separate access record is the stronger option when ac

## Intent

Give each actor type its own view of the same domain entities. An administrator sees the full entity. A restricted actor sees a smaller transfer object with fewer fields, read-only derived fields and only the access it needs.

[4481] TOOLRESULT: ---
id: invitation-with-key-based-acceptance

aliases: [invite link, key invitation, anonymous invitation acceptance]

status: emerging

first_seen: 2026-03-05
last_updated: 2026-11-10
---
## Intent

Let someone who has no account yet accept an invitation. The invitation is an entity with an email, an expiration and a key. The key is carried in a link, so the invitee can open the link and accept without first signing in. On the platform this is an actor marked anonymous (an unauthenticated guest) whose access reaches the link transfer object; the key scheme itself is project practice, not a platform feature. Accepting or cancelling is an operation on the invitation, and the link itself is a transfer object that exposes only what the invitee needs.

### The key is the access credential

fragment: approval-workflow-pattern
diagram: { kinds: [entity, operation], members: types }
last_updated: 2026-11-10
---
## Intent

A value must be proposed by one party and confirmed by another before it counts. Keep the proposed and the approved value side by side on the same entity, derive the approval status from the two, and make approval an operation that copies one to the other.

## Practice

### Two parallel members, one derived status

- The entity has two optional relations (0..1) to the same target, here `requestedLevel` and `approvedLevel`. The requester edits the first; only the approve operation writes the second.
- A derived boolean `approved` is true when both are set and equal. Because it is DERIVED it is always in step with the data and needs no storage.
- A parent can expose a derived boo

## Intent

A form field should be mandatory only when another field has a certain value (for example a name that is required only for active records). The rule lives in a frontend action hook, so the generated page stays untouched.

## Practice

### Platform base

- The generator emits per-page and per-container hook points. A page hook has the key `<PAGE>_ACTIONS_HOOK_INTERFACE_KEY` and the type `<Container>ActionsHook`, both exported from the page's generated `customization` module (dialogs: `~/dialogs/<path>/customization`). [4500] TOOLRESULT: alternatives: []
related: [price-margin-calculation-utilities, registry-header-with-lines, draft-done-status-enum]
fragment: quote-lines-with-price-modifiers
diagram: { kinds: [entity, enum], members: types }
first_seen: 2026-09-24
last_updated: 2026-11-10
---
## Intent

Price a quote from its lines and let discounts and surcharges act at two levels. A line carries a quantity and a price. A price modifier can be attached to one line, or to the whole quote. The totals are derived, so they never drift from the lines.

### A modifier has a catalog definition and an applied instance

The modifier definition holds the name, the percentage, the kind (surcharge or discount) and the scope (line or quote). The applied instance points to a definition and carries the percentage used on this quote. A later change of the definition does not rewrite quotes that already used it.

### The scope says where an instance may sit

A modifier whose scope is line is attached to a line. A modifier whose scope is quote is attached to the quote itself. The scope enumeration is the rule that decides which owner is allowed.

## Structure

- **Quote** has a stored `status` (draft, done, accepted, rejected), a composition `lines` to many QuoteLine, a composition `modifiers` to many PriceModifierInstance, and a derived `total`.
- **QuoteLine** has an integer `quantity`, a numeric `basePrice`, a mandatory association `product` to Product, a composition `modifiers` to many PriceModifierInstance, and a derive

[4541] TOOLRESULT: ## Backend guidance

### Generate the key with a secure random source

The key in the link must be random and unguessable. Generate it with a cryptographically secure random source, and make it long enough to resist guessing. Do not derive it from the email or a counter.

```java
String key = secureRandomAlphanumeric(32);
```

### Create invitations in a dedicated operation

Create the Invitation in a custom operation that sets the type, the email, an expiration, and the key. Send the email after the invitation is saved, so a failed save does not send a link.

### Check the key and the expiration on accept

On accept, load the invitation by its key, refuse the call if the key is unknown, and refuse it if the expiration has passed. Then branch on the invitation type: USER creates a user, ACCOUNT adds the existing account to the organization.

### Check the password confirmation in the operation

The accept operation checks that the password and its confirmation match before it creates anything. Report a mismatch as a field error: throw `ValidationException` (HTTP 400) with the attribute as location, not a business fault (422).

### Delete the invitation after acceptance

Delete the invitation in the same transaction that creates the user or the membership. A failure then rolls back the whole acceptance, and the link stays usable. This relies on the operation running in one transaction; verify that for the project's custom operation setup.

### Cancel only pending invitations

import { useJudoNavigation } from '~/components';

  CART_INFO_CART_INFO_VIEW_EDIT_CHECKOUT_INPUT_FORM_ACTIONS_HOOK_INTERFACE_KEY,
  type CheckoutInputCheckoutInput_FormActionsHook,
} from '~/dialogs/CartInfo/CartInfo_View_Edit/Checkout/Input/Form/customization';
import { routeToCustomerOrdersAccessViewPage } from '~/routes';
import { CustomerServiceForOrdersImpl } from '~/services/data-axios/CustomerServiceForOrdersImpl';
import { judoAxiosProvider } from '~/services/data-axios/JudoAxiosProvider';

// After a successful checkout the new order opens: the newest order of the customer is the one just placed.
// (The operation returns nothing: a mapped output type makes the generator emit duplicate imports, GAPS.md R8-5.)
const checkoutHook: CheckoutInputCheckoutInput_FormActionsHook = () => {
  const { navigate } = useJudoNavigation();
  return {
    postCheckoutForCartInfoAction: async (onSubmit) => {
      await onSubmit();
      const service = new CustomerServiceForOrdersImpl(judoAxiosProvider);
      const response = await service.list(undefined, {
        _mask: '{number}',
        _orderBy: [{ attribute: 'orderedAt', descending: true }],
      } as any);
      const newest = response.data[0];
      if (newest) {
        navigate(routeToCustomerOrdersAccessViewPage(newest.__signedIdentifier));
      }
    },
  };
};

  context.registerService<C

[4731] TOOLRESULT:  * effect of the business operation, inside its transaction (best-practices/model/notification-entity-pattern.md).

@Component(immediate = true, service = NotificationService.class)
public class NotificationService {

    @Reference CustomerAccountDao customerAccountDao;
    @Reference SalesOrderDao salesOrderDao;

    public void orderShipped(SalesOrder order, String trackingNumber) {
        CustomerAccount customer = salesOrderDao.queryCustomer(order, CustomerAccountMask.customerAccountMask().withEmail());
        customerAccountDao.createNotifications(customer, NotificationForCreate.builder()
                .withTitle("Your order has shipped")
                .withMessage("Order " + order.getNumber() + " is on its way. Tracking number: " + trackingNumber)
                .withOrderNumber(order.getNumber())
                .withCreatedAt(BusinessClock.now())
                .withRead(false)
                .build());
    }
}

  CUSTOMER_CART_ACCESS_VIEW_PAGE_ACTIONS_HOOK_INTERFACE_KEY,

// F36: the cart summary is one custom component that loads its own values. After "Apply coupon" / "Remove coupon"
// the generated page does not hand it new data, so the post-operation hooks (agent-docs/frontend/hooks/action-hooks.md)
// tell it to load again.
export const CART_CHANGED_EVENT = 'webshop:cart-changed';
const cartChanged = () => window.dispatchEvent(new Event(CART_CHANGED_EVENT));

const applyHook: CouponInputCouponInput_FormActionsHook = () => ({
  postApplyCouponForCartInfoAction: async (onSubmit) => {
    await onSubmit();
    cartChanged();
  },
});

const cartPageHook: CartInfoCartInfo_View_EditActionsHook = (_data, _editMode, _storeDiff, refresh) => ({
  postRemoveCouponForCartInfoAction: async () => {
    await refresh();
    cartChanged();
  },
});

// The shop front: actor Customer (F14). Users: customer1/customer1 and customer2/customer2
// (scripts/seed-dev-user.sh); anybody can sign up (F13). The password equals the user name.
export async function login(page: Page, user = 'customer1') {
  await apiHelper.gotoWithWait(page); // app start URL, redirects to Keycloak
  await expect(page.locator('#kc-form-login')).toBeVisible({ timeout: 15000 });
  await page.locator('#username').fill(user);
  await page.locator('#password').fill(user);
  await page.locator('#kc-login').click();
  await expect(page.getByTestId('catalog-grid').or(page.getByTestId('catalog-empty'))).toBeVisible({ timeout: 30000 }); // the shop home is the card grid
}

async function newCustomer(browser: Browser, user: string, first = 'Rita', last = 'Reviewer') {
  const context = await browser.newContext({ viewport: { width: 1440, height: 900 } });
  const page = await context.newPage();
  await signUp(page, user, first, last);
  return { page, context };
}

  await expect(page.getByRole('dialog')).toBeVisible();
}

export async function submitCreateDialog(page: Page) {
  await apiHelper.triggerAndWaitWithRegex(page, page.getByRole('dialog').getByRole('button', { name: /Create$/ }), /.*(list|~create)$/);
}

export async function createCategory(page: Page, name: string) {
  await openCreateDialog(page);
  const box = page.getByRole('dialog').getByRole('textbox', { name: /Name/ });
  await box.click();
  await box.pressSequentially(name, { delay: 3 }); // real key events: fill() does not mark the generated form as changed
  await box.blur();
  await page.waitForTimeout(700); // generated text fields store the value with a debounce (DebouncedTextField)
  await submitCreateDialog(page);
  await expect(page.getByRole('dialog')).toBeHidden();
  await expect(page.getByRole('gridcell', { name })).toBeVisible();
}
